          🌊 Core Features

          • 📗Updated Minecraft to 1.21.10.
          • 📗The World Border's normal color is now the map theme color.
          • 📙The term "border level" was renamed to "Border Energy".
          • 📙Border upgrades can now consume up to 40 experience levels.
          • 📙Raised the experience cost of border upgrades between levels 10 and 100.
          • 📙At 7-9 Border Energy, a border upgrade now costs 1 experience level.
          • 📙Running /function border_survival:get_sign now yields a sign made of the map theme material instead of oak.
          • 📙Monsters now chase a World Bug target within 320 blocks horizontally, up from 256.
          • 📙Evokers now chase a World Bug target within 320 blocks both horizontally and vertically.
          • 🐞Fixed the World Border in 1.21.9 no longer keeping all three dimensions the same size.
          • 🐞Fixed odd portal CDs in the Nether and the End.
          • 🐞Fixed broken retaliation behavior when monsters chase a World Bug target.
          • 🐞Fixed monsters chasing a World Bug target only trying to switch to the nearest player when several players are nearby.

          🌗 Day/Night Mode

          • 📗The Day/Night Mode daytime border color is no longer yellow, but the map theme color.
          • 📕Nights now expand the play area twofold at 50 Border Energy, down from 100.

          🌙 Eternal Night

          • 📙Eternal Nights now arrive just before dawn at 50 Border Energy, down from 100.
          • 📙The World Border no longer expands twofold during an Eternal Night.
          • 📙Eternal Nights are no longer attempted in Peaceful Mode.
          • 📙Monster armor chance: 100% -> 50%.
          • 📙Monster armor quality no longer improves and reverts to vanilla behavior.
          • 🐞Fixed broken retaliation between Evernight Knights.
          • 🐞Fixed the knight count going wrong after a Zombie Knight turned into a Drowned.

          🐎 Evernight Knights

          • 📙Evernight Knights now prioritize nearby players even when chasing or retaliating against non-player mobs.
          • 📙Illusioner King arrow effect duration: 10s -> random 5-10s.
          • 📙Eternal Divine Weapons drop chance: 15% per Looting level -> 12% per Looting level.

          📚 Crafting

          • 📗Added recipes for Copper Golem, Iron Golem, and Snow Golem spawn eggs.
          • 📗They unlock automatically when a Copper Ingot, Iron Ingot, or Snowball is obtained.
          • 📙Monster Spawners now craft from Obsidian x6 + Diamond x2 + Trapped Chest x1.

          🔆 Chaos Smith

          • 📗A death message is now sent when a Chaos Smith dies.
          • 📗Added Unbreakable Spirit Copper and the Copper Spirit equipment series.
          • 📗Unbreakable Spirit Copper costs Chaos Star x1 + Block of Copper x8.
          • 📗Consuming a block of Unbreakable Spirit Wood / Stone / Copper / Iron / Gold / Diamond / Netherite now grants 10/10/15/20/30/50/100 Border Energy.
          • 📗A block of Unbreakable Spirit Netherite can now be handed to a Chaos Smith to return an Eternal Divine Weapon to its origin as a Night Spirit Eternal Weapon.
          • 📗A wolf wearing Spirit Wolf Armor now gains Resistance continuously and an Instant Health every 5 seconds.
          • 📙Spirit Wolf Armor costs Unbreakable Spirit Gold x1 + Armadillo Scute x6 -> Unbreakable Spirit Gold x6 + Armadillo Scute x6.
          • 📙Spiritguard Shield absorption duration: 20s -> 30s.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Equipment trades are no longer affected by reputation.

          🐈 Sustainer

          • 📗Sustainers are no longer exclusive to Night Garden.
          • 📗Sustainers hold up the World Border's energy. Border Energy drains when no Sustainer is near the world spawn point, down to 7.
          • 📗Sustainers generate at the world spawn point and try to return there when they stray too far.
          • 📗A Sustainer that dies reappears at the world spawn point after a while.
          • 📗When a Chaos Smith is nearby, Sustainers heal every nearby player every 5 seconds.
          • 📗At the same time, since a Sustainer is a World Bug (bug), all monsters want to destroy it.
          • 📗Sustainers do not follow their owner, do not travel through Nether or End portals, and do not ride in boats or minecarts.
          • 📗Name Tags cannot be used on Sustainers.

          🐎 永夜骑士

          • 现在永夜骑士在追击/反击非玩家生物时，也会优先攻击附近的玩家。

          🐈 12.0-rc1 1.21.10 2025-12-0912.0-rc1 1.21.10 2025-12-0912.0-rc1 1.21.10 2025-12-09

          本次包含破坏性更新，不兼容1.21.4版本存档存留的特殊物品，请将它们消耗完后再进行存档升级。

          🌊 核心特性

          • 📗Minecraft升级至1.21.10。
          • 📗现在世界边界的默认颜色是地图的主题色。
          • 📙名词「边界等级」调整为「边界能量」。
          • 📙现在边界升级消耗的经验等级最高可提升至40级。
          • 📙提升了10-100级之间进行边界升级消耗的经验等级。
          • 📙现在执行 /function border_survival:get_sign 指令会获得与地图主题相同材质的功能牌，不再是橡木功能牌。
          • 📙「流浪商人生成提醒」和「破坏性行为规则修复」不再是独立模组。
          • 🐞修复了1.21.9世界边界不再三维度大小同步的问题。
          • 🐞修复了下界、末地传送门CD异常的问题。

          🌗 昼夜模式

          • 📗现在昼夜模式白天的边界颜色不再是黄色，而是地图的主题色。
          • 📕现在当边界能量至少拥有50级时，夜晚将会扩展一倍空间，不再是100级。

          🌙 永恒之夜

          • 📗现在永夜骑士每30秒会窃取一级边界能量，并转化为其自身的1点生命值上限，每多存在一位永夜骑士，窃取频率提高2秒，若边界能量小于等于50级，将会停止窃取。
          • 📗击败所有永夜骑士后，被永夜骑士窃取的边界能量将会自动归还。
          • 📙现在永恒之夜会在边界能量至少有50级，即将白昼的时刻降临，不再是100级。
          • 📙现在处于永恒之夜状态下，世界边界不再额外扩展一倍。
          • 📙现在处于和平模式状态下，不再尝试进入永恒之夜。
          • 📙现在幻术厄王的药箭效果持续时间为随机5-10秒，不再是10秒。
          • 📙永夜骑士基础生命值上限：100点 -> 50点。
          • 📙永恒神兵掉落概率：15%/抢夺等级 -> 12%/抢夺等级。
          • 📙怪物穿戴装甲概率：100% -> 50%。
          • 📙怪物穿戴的装甲品质不再提升，回滚为原版行为。
          • 🐞修复了永夜骑士之间的反击行为异常的问题。
          • 🐞修复了僵尸骑士转化为溺尸后，骑士计数异常的问题。
          • 🐞修复了怪物在追击世界之虫目标时反击行为异常的问题。
          • 🐞修复了当怪物在追击世界之虫目标，且附近存在多个玩家时，只会尝试转火最近玩家的问题。

          🐈 边界维系者

          • 📗维系者不再是夜屿苍园独有特性。
          • 📗维系者维系着世界边界的能量，当世界出生点周围不存在维系者时，边界能量将会持续流失，最低流失至7级，处于永恒之夜状态下时，最低流失至50级。
          • 📗维系者会生成在世界出生点，若维系者距离世界出生点过远，会尝试回到世界出生点。
          • 📗因维系者为世界之虫(bug)，所有怪物都会试图消灭祂。
          • 📗维系者被击杀后，一段时间过后会重新降生于世界出生点。
          • 📗当维系者附近存在神匠时，维系者每5秒会治疗一次附近的所有玩家。
          • 📗维系者不会跟随主人，不会穿越下界传送门和末地传送门，不会搭载船或矿车。
          • 📗无法对维系者使用命名牌。

          本次含毀物之更，不相容1.21.4版存檔所存之特殊之物，請盡其用盡而後再行生界之昇。

          🌊 核心特性

          • 📗Minecraft升至1.21.10。
          • 📗世疆之常色今為輿圖之主題色。
          • 📙名「疆階」已調為「疆尺」。
          • 📙今世疆晉階所耗之經驗之階，至多可升至四十階。
          • 📙已增十至一百階間行世疆晉階所耗之經驗之階。
          • 📙今行 /function border_survival:get_sign 令者，得與輿圖主題同材之藝能牌，不復為柞木藝能牌。
          • 📙「流商生成提醒」與「毀物之則修復」不復為獨立之機。
          • 🐞已修1.21.9世疆不復三界之大小相齊之弊。
          • 🐞已修焱界、終界結界門CD異常之弊。

          🌗 晝夜之制

          • 📗今晝夜之制晝時之世疆色不復為黃，乃輿圖之主題色。
          • 📕今疆尺至少百階時，夜將拓一倍之地，不復為一百階。

          🌙 永恆之夜

          • 📗今長夜騎士每三十息竊一階疆尺，化為其自身一點命極；每多一長夜騎士，竊之頻增二息；若疆尺小於等於五十階，則止竊。
          • 📗誅盡長夜騎士後，被其竊之疆尺將自動歸還。
          • 📙今永恆之夜將於疆尺至少五十階、將晝之刻降臨，不復為一百階。
          • 📙今處永恆之夜之態時，世疆不復額拓一倍。
          • 📙今處和之制之態時，不復圖入永恆之夜。
          • 📙今幻術厄王之藥矢之效恆久為隨機五至十息，不復為十息。
          • 📙長夜騎士基命極：一百點 -> 五十點。
          • 📙永恆神兵墜之機：每階十五分 -> 每階十二分。
          • 📙敵獸衣甲之機：十成 -> 五成。
          • 📙敵獸所衣之甲品不復升，退為原世之行。
          • 🐞已修長夜騎士之間反擊之行異常之弊。
          • 🐞已修殭屍冥騎化為溺屍後，騎之計數異常之弊。
          • 🐞已修敵獸追界之蠹之所在時反擊之行異常之弊。
          • 🐞已修敵獸追界之蠹之所在，且近旁有多戲者時，唯圖轉擊最近戲者之弊。

          📚 製物譜

          • 📗已增銅傀儡、鐵傀儡、雪傀儡孳卵之譜。
          • 📗得銅錠、鐵錠、雪團者，自動解其對應之譜。
          • 📙孳衍籠譜中之鐵檻已改為鐵錠。

          🔆 天工

          • 📗已增無瑕靈銅、銅靈系列之器。
          • 📗易無瑕靈銅所需之物：聚靈石x1 + 銅塊八枚。
          • 📗今可耗一塊無瑕靈椿／石／銅／鐵／金／金剛／玄鈺，增疆尺三／一／二／五／十／五十／百階。
          • 📗今可耗一塊無瑕玄鈺，託天工滌永恆神兵之滅咒，使其晉為夜靈神兵。
          • 📗今天工斃時，會發斃信。
          • 📗今狼衣狼靈甲時，恆得堅之效，同每五息得瞬療一次。
          • 📙易狼靈甲所需之物：無瑕靈金x1 + 犰狳鱗x6 -> 無瑕靈金x6 + 犰狳鱗x6。
          • 📙易無瑕靈椿所需之物：鴻蒙珠x1 + 柞木x64 -> 聚靈石x9 + 柞木六十四枚。
          • 📙易無瑕靈石所需之物：鴻蒙珠x1 + 石x64 -> 聚靈石x1 + 石六十四枚。
          • 📙易無瑕靈鐵所需之物：鴻蒙珠x1 + 鐵塊x9 -> 聚靈石x9 + 鐵塊八枚。
          • 📙易無瑕靈金所需之物：鴻蒙珠x1 + 金塊x9 -> 聚靈石x18 + 金塊八枚。
          • 📙易無瑕金剛所需之物：鴻蒙珠x1 + 金剛石塊x4 -> 聚靈石x27 + 金剛石塊八枚。
          • 📙易無瑕玄鈺所需之物：鴻蒙珠x1 + 玄鈺塊x1 -> 鴻蒙珠九枚 + 玄鈺塊一枚。
          • 📙御靈盾消災恆久：二十息 -> 三十息。
          • 📙無瑕靈器之商項不復受聲望所制（不復打折）。

          🐈 維者

          • 📗維者不復為夜嶼蒼園所獨有之特性。
          • 📗維者維世疆之尺；界生處之旁無維者時，疆尺將恆流散，最低流至七階；處永恆之夜之態時，最低流至五十階。
          • 📗維者生於界生處；若維者距界生處過遠，則圖歸界生處。
          • 📗以維者為界之蠹（bug），諸敵獸皆欲滅之。
          • 📗維者為戲者所誅後，一陣之後重降於界生處。
          • 📗維者之旁有天工時，維者每五息療近旁諸戲者一次。
          • 📗維者不從主，不穿焱界結界門與終界結界門，不乘舟或礦車。
          • 📗弗能用名刺於維者。

          This update contains breaking changes. Saves from 1.21.4 that still hold special items are incompatible: consume them before upgrading the save.

          🌊 Core Features

          • 📗Updated Minecraft to 1.21.10.
          • 📗The World Border's default color is now the map theme color.
          • 📙The term "border level" was renamed to "Border Energy".
          • 📙Border upgrades can now consume up to 40 experience levels.
          • 📙Raised the experience cost of border upgrades between levels 10 and 100.
          • 📙Running /function border_survival:get_sign now yields a sign made of the map theme material instead of oak.
          • 📙"Wandering Trader Spawn Reminder" and "Griefing Behavior Rule Fix" are no longer separate mods.
          • 🐞Fixed the World Border in 1.21.9 no longer keeping all three dimensions the same size.
          • 🐞Fixed odd portal CDs in the Nether and the End.

          🌗 Day/Night Mode

          • 📗The Day/Night Mode daytime border color is no longer yellow, but the map theme color.
          • 📕Nights now expand the play area twofold at 50 Border Energy, down from 100.

          🌙 Eternal Night

          • 📗Evernight Knights now siphon 1 Border Energy every 30 seconds and convert it into 1 max health for themselves. Each extra Evernight Knight slows the siphon by 2 seconds, and siphoning stops at 50 Border Energy or below.
          • 📗After all Evernight Knights are defeated, the Border Energy they siphoned is returned automatically.
          • 📙Eternal Nights now arrive just before dawn at 50 Border Energy, down from 100.
          • 📙The World Border no longer expands twofold during an Eternal Night.
          • 📙Eternal Nights are no longer attempted in Peaceful Mode.
          • 📙Illusioner King arrow effect duration: random 5-10s, down from 10s.
          • 📙Evernight Knight base max health: 100 -> 50.
          • 📙Eternal Divine Weapons drop chance: 15% per Looting level -> 12% per Looting level.
          • 📙Monster armor chance: 100% -> 50%.
          • 📙Monster armor quality no longer improves and reverts to vanilla behavior.
          • 🐞Fixed broken retaliation between Evernight Knights.
          • 🐞Fixed the knight count going wrong after a Zombie Knight turned into a Drowned.
          • 🐞Fixed broken retaliation when monsters chase a World Bug target.
          • 🐞Fixed monsters chasing a World Bug target only trying to switch to the nearest player when several players are nearby.

          📚 Crafting

          • 📗Added recipes for Copper Golem, Iron Golem, and Snow Golem spawn eggs.
          • 📗They unlock automatically when a Copper Ingot, Iron Ingot, or Snowball is obtained.
          • 📙The Monster Spawner recipe now uses Iron Ingots instead of Iron Bars.

          🔆 Chaos Smith

          • 📗Added Unbreakable Spirit Copper and the Copper Spirit equipment series.
          • 📗Unbreakable Spirit Copper costs Spirit Emerald x1 + Block of Copper x8.
          • 📗Consuming a block of Unbreakable Spirit Wood / Stone / Copper / Iron / Gold / Diamond / Netherite now grants 3/1/2/5/10/50/100 Border Energy.
          • 📗A block of Unbreakable Spirit Netherite can now be handed to a Chaos Smith to wash the Curse of Vanishing off an Eternal Divine Weapon, upgrading it to a Night Spirit Eternal Weapon.
          • 📗A death message is now sent when a Chaos Smith dies.
          • 📗A wolf wearing Spirit Wolf Armor now gains Resistance continuously and an Instant Health every 5 seconds.
          • 📙Spirit Wolf Armor costs Unbreakable Spirit Gold x1 + Armadillo Scute x6 -> Unbreakable Spirit Gold x6 + Armadillo Scute x6.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Wood costs Chaos Star x1 + Oak Wood x64 -> Spirit Emerald x9 + Oak Wood x64.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Stone costs Chaos Star x1 + Stone x64 -> Spirit Emerald x1 + Stone x64.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Iron costs Chaos Star x1 + Block of Iron x9 -> Spirit Emerald x9 + Block of Iron x8.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Gold costs Chaos Star x1 + Block of Gold x9 -> Spirit Emerald x18 + Block of Gold x8.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Diamond costs Chaos Star x1 + Block of Diamond x4 -> Spirit Emerald x27 + Block of Diamond x8.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Netherite costs Chaos Star x1 + Block of Netherite x1 -> Chaos Star x9 + Block of Netherite x1.
          • 📙Spiritguard Shield absorption duration: 20s -> 30s.
          • 📙Unbreakable Spirit Equipment trades are no longer affected by reputation (no longer discounted).

          🐈 Sustainer

          • 📗Sustainers are no longer exclusive to Night Garden.
          • 📗Sustainers hold up the World Border's energy. Border Energy drains continuously when no Sustainer is near the world spawn point, down to 7, or down to 50 during an Eternal Night.
          • 📗Sustainers generate at the world spawn point and try to return there when they stray too far.
          • 📗Since a Sustainer is a World Bug (bug), all monsters try to destroy it.
          • 📗A Sustainer that is defeated reappears at the world spawn point after a while.
          • 📗When a Chaos Smith is nearby, Sustainers heal every nearby player every 5 seconds.
          • 📗Sustainers do not follow their owner, do not travel through Nether or End portals, and do not ride in boats or minecarts.
          • 📗Name Tags cannot be used on Sustainers.

          💎 Loot Chests

          • 📙Lowered the chance of structure loot in the ???.

          🎣 Fishing

          • 📙Lowered the weight of ??? in the treasure loot pool.

          🐱 Cat

          • 📙Removed from gifts: Ice, Packed Ice, Blue Ice.

          🌸 Advancements

          • 📗Added the The Age of Spirit Copper advancement.
          • 📗Added the Copper-Tempered, Wash Away the Curse, and Glory of the Evernight challenge advancements.
          • 📙Adjusted the completion conditions, names, and descriptions of the Border Energy goal advancements.
          • 📙Added usage hints to most advancement descriptions.
          • 📙Adjusted the parent-child relations of some advancements.

          📄 Localization

          • 📗The names and descriptions of all advancements, signs, spirit items, and hints are now localization keys.
          • 📗Added localization for zh_cn and en_us.

          🌙 11.18.1 1.21.4 2025-03-2011.18.1 1.21.4 2025-03-2011.18.1 1.21.4 2025-03-20

          🔆 神匠

          • 现在当物品实体周围存在多个有效聚灵石时，若该物品实体的堆叠数量不是有效聚灵石数量的倍数，余下的物品数量将会被随机聚集。

          • When both a general and a container Spirit Emerald are around the same item entity, the item is now pulled in by the general one first.
          • After being pulled in by a general Spirit Emerald, an item entity now tries to merge on the next tick, and is then pulled in again by a valid container Spirit Emerald if one is nearby.

          • Fixed the Illager Slayer and Final Judgment hidden challenge advancements not appearing on the advancement screen after completion.

          🌙 11.18 1.21.4 2025-03-1811.18 1.21.4 2025-03-1811.18 1.21.4 2025-03-18

          🧊 永恒雨夜 / 永恒雪夜

          • 新增永恒雨夜/永恒雪夜事件：当永恒之夜降临时，永夜骑士会汲取世界边界的力量转化为其自身的生命值。根据汲取程度，世界边界将会染上灿金、夕橙、邃蓝、黯紫、凶红五种色彩。每当累计度过三次永恒之夜后，下次永恒之夜将会降临亡灵军团或灾厄军团。每当累计度过八次永恒之夜后，下次永恒之夜将会降临永夜军团。当任意永恒军团降临后，世界边界将会染上苍白。若永恒之夜状态下的世界边界处于灿金、夕橙、邃蓝状态，所有生物群系都将异常降雨，无论温暖或寒冷。若永恒之夜状态下的世界边界处于黯紫、凶红、苍白状态时，所有生物群系都将异常降雪，无论寒冷或温暖。在所有降临的永夜骑士均被击败后，异常气候将会恢复正常，世界边界的色彩将随游戏日逐渐恢复，并会以色彩预示下次永恒之夜的降临时刻。

          🌙 永恒之夜

          • 现在夜骑·幻翼拥有40点生命值上限，不再是20点。
          • 现在永夜骑士将有一定概率进行组队降临，不会在第一次永恒之夜中组队，第一次永恒之夜不会出现凶红之夜。

          🌸 进度

          • 新增「亡灵杀手」、「灾厄杀手」、「终劫」系列隐藏挑战进度，达成后将分别获得100/100/200级经验值。
          • 「万骑聚荡」隐藏挑战进度达成后现在将获得200级经验值，不再是1000级。

          🧊 永恆雨夜 / 永恆雪夜

          • 已增永恆雨夜／永恆雪夜之事：永恆之夜降臨時，長夜騎士汲取世疆之力，化為其自身命值。依汲取之度，世疆將染燦金、夕橙、邃藍、黯紫、煞紅五色。每累度過三次永恆之夜後，下次永恆之夜將降殭屍軍團或災厄軍團。每累度過八次永恆之夜後，下次永恆之夜將降長夜軍團。任何永恆軍團降後，世疆將染素縞。若永恆之夜之世疆處燦金、夕橙、邃藍之態，諸生態域皆異常降雨，無論溫暖或寒涼。若永恆之夜之世疆處黯紫、煞紅、素縞之態，諸生態域皆異常降雪，無論寒涼或溫暖。諸降之長夜騎士盡誅之後，異常之氣象將復其常，世疆之色將隨戲日漸復，並以色示下次永恆之夜之降臨之刻。

          🌙 永恆之夜

          • 今夜騎·魘靈有四十點命極，不復為二十點。
          • 今長夜騎士將有一定之機結隊而降，不於第一次永恆之夜結隊；第一次永恆之夜不現煞紅之夜。

          🌸 進程

          • 已增「殭軍殺伐」、「厄軍殺伐」、「終劫」系列隱秘挑戰進程，達後分別得一百／一百／二百階經驗。
          • 「萬騎聚蕩」隱秘挑戰進程達後今得二百階經驗，不復為一千階。

          🧊 Eternal Rain Night / Eternal Snow Night

          • Added the Eternal Rain Night and Eternal Snow Night events: when an Eternal Night arrives, Evernight Knights draw on the World Border's power and convert it into health for themselves. Depending on how much they draw, the World Border takes on Bright Gold, Sun Orange, Deep Blue, Dark Purple, or Danger Red. After every three Eternal Nights, the next Eternal Night brings an Undead Legion or an Illager Legion. After every eight Eternal Nights, the next Eternal Night brings an Evernight Legion. Once any Eternal Legion has arrived, the World Border takes on Pale White. While the World Border shows Bright Gold, Sun Orange, or Deep Blue during an Eternal Night, every biome rains regardless of warmth. While it shows Dark Purple, Danger Red, or Pale White, every biome snows regardless of cold. Once every Evernight Knight that arrived has been defeated, the weather returns to normal, the World Border's color recovers over the following game days, and that color signals when the next Eternal Night will arrive.

          🌙 Eternal Night

          • Phantom Riders now have 40 max health, up from 20.
          • Evernight Knights now have a chance of arriving in a group, and never group up on the first Eternal Night, which also never shows a Danger Red night.

          🌸 Advancements

          • Added the Smite, Illager Slayer, and Final Judgment hidden challenge advancements, granting 100/100/200 experience levels respectively.
          • Completing the A Thousand Riders Rampage hidden challenge advancement now grants 200 experience levels, down from 1000.

          • 緣即刻生效之效其恆久不在噴劑中生效，今聖靈泉、魔靈泉之瞬療Ⅱ、瞬傷Ⅱ不復具恆久。
          • 聖靈泉飽和之效已調：飽和Ⅰ -> 飽和Ⅹ。
          • 🐞 已修剔透置具匡中物之淬靈之光效散失之弊。

          🌸 進程

          • 已調「此為何物？」「此又是何物？」「？？？」「永恆之侶？」系列進程之名色。

          • Items that can be pulled in by a Spirit Emerald are now pulled in by the nearest one first.
          • The Demonic Spirit Spring and Sacred Spirit Spring descriptions are now "Demonic Spirit Spring" and "Sacred Spirit Spring".
          • Because instant effects do not carry their duration through splash potions, the Instant Health Ⅱ and Instant Damage Ⅱ effects of the Sacred and Demonic Spirit Springs no longer have a duration.

          • 🐞 Fixed the missing enchantment glint on items in Invisible Item Frames.

          • The "]" key now toggles tweakeroo's fast block placement.

          🌙 11.14 1.21.4 2025-02-2511.14 1.21.4 2025-02-2511.14 1.21.4 2025-02-25

          🌊 核心特性

          • 现在由怪物产生的箭矢只会存在8秒，不再是60秒。
          • 🐞 修复了实体进入传送门的CD异常问题。

          🔆 神匠

          • 新增物品：聚灵石，替代绿灵石，可通过怪物掉落物兑换。
          • 聚灵箱现在需要使用9个聚灵石兑换。
          • 当聚灵石被放置在物品展示框时，将聚集周围掉落物。
          • 当聚灵石所处的物品展示框被附着在箱子、木桶、潜影盒上时，将聚集周围在其附着容器中存在的同种类掉落物。若容器为空，将聚集周围所有类型掉落物。
          • 当一个掉落物实体周围存在多颗聚灵石时，掉落物将分裂成若干个实体被这些聚灵石平均聚集。
          • 当一个掉落物实体周围分别存在仅限聚集容器内物品的聚灵石与可聚集所有掉落物的聚灵石时，优先被前者聚集。
          • 混沌之星特殊效果调整：力量Ⅱ -> 生命恢复Ⅱ。
          • 御灵盾特殊效果CD调整：30s -> 20s。
          • 魔灵泉瞬间伤害Ⅱ持续时间调整：1tick -> 30tick。
          • 圣灵泉瞬间治疗Ⅱ持续时间调整：1tick -> 30tick。

          🌙 永恒之夜

          • 现在当永恒之夜降临后，将在度过0-4个游戏日后再次降临，不再是0-6个游戏日。
          • 现在当永恒之夜降临时，世界出生点周围将随机出现除溺尸国王和幻术厄王以外的永夜骑士。
          • 现在累计降临三次永恒之夜后，第四次永恒之夜将随机降临以溺尸国王为首的亡灵军团或以幻术厄王为首的灾厄军团。
          • 现在累计降临八次永恒之夜后，第九次永恒之夜将降临永夜军团。
          • 在一轮永夜军团中，亡灵军团和灾厄军团都会先后降临一次。

          🌾 永夜骑士

          • 新增灾厄骑士成员：卫道斧士。
          • 新增亡灵骑士成员：沼骸骑士。
          • 新增物品：永恒之心，在深海瑰宝战利品中有50%的概率替代海洋之心。
          • 使用永恒之心使其破碎后，在本次或下次的亡灵军团、灾厄军团、永夜军团中，永夜骑士将不再团结一心。该效果可累计叠加。
          • 现在被永夜骑士击败的生物视为被玩家击败。
          • 幻术法师现在的名称为幻术厄王。
          • 幻术厄王现在拥有200点基础生命值，不再是100点。
          • 幻术厄王现在会射出随机状态效果Ⅰ级的药箭（不包含征兆、村庄英雄、寄生），状态效果持续10s。
          • 幻术厄王现在被玩家击败后，将会掉落一个深海瑰宝。
          • 现在夜骑·劫掠兽、夜骑·幻翼被玩家击败后，将会掉落10个聚灵箱，每级抢夺额外增加0-2个。
          • 溺尸国王现在会在世界出生点周围的天空上生成。

          • 优化了所有破坏性规则的逻辑。
          • 🐞 修复了在creeperGriefing(苦力怕破坏性)关闭的情况下，物品展示框中的物品仍能被箭矢击落的问题。

          🔆 Chaos Smith

          • Added an item: the Spirit Emerald, which replaces the Emerald and can be traded for monster drops.
          • The Bonus Chest now costs 9 Spirit Emeralds.
          • Placing a Spirit Emerald in an item frame makes it pull in nearby drops.
          • When the item frame holding a Spirit Emerald is attached to a chest, barrel, or shulker box, it pulls in nearby drops of the same kind stored in that container. When the container is empty, it pulls in every kind of nearby drop.
          • When several Spirit Emeralds are around a drop, the drop splits into several entities that they pull in evenly.
          • When a container-only Spirit Emerald and an all-drop Spirit Emerald are both around the same drop, the drop is pulled in by the container-only one first.

          • After an Eternal Night arrives, the next one now arrives 0-4 game days later, down from 0-6.
          • When an Eternal Night arrives, Evernight Knights other than the Drowned King and Illusioner King now spawn randomly around the world spawn point.
          • After three Eternal Nights have arrived, the fourth now brings a random Undead Legion led by the Drowned King or an Illager Legion led by the Illusioner King.
          • After eight Eternal Nights have arrived, the ninth now brings an Evernight Legion.
          • Within one round of Evernight Legions, both the Undead Legion and the Illager Legion arrive once each.

          • Added an Illager Knight: the Vindicator Blademaster.
          • Added an Undead Knight: the Bogged Knight.
          • Added an item: the Eternal Heart, which has a 50% chance of replacing the Heart of the Sea in deep-sea treasure loot.
          • Breaking an Eternal Heart makes the Evernight Knights stop fighting as one during the current or next Undead Legion, Illager Legion, or Evernight Legion. This effect stacks.
          • Mobs defeated by Evernight Knights now count as defeated by a player.
          • The Illusioner is now called the Illusioner King.
          • The Illusioner King now has 200 base health, up from 100.
          • The Illusioner King now shoots tipped arrows with a random tier Ⅰ status effect, excluding Omen, Hero of the Village, and Infested, lasting 10s.
          • The Illusioner King now drops a deep-sea treasure when defeated by a player.
          • Defeating a Ravager Rider or Phantom Rider as a player now drops 10 Bonus Chests, with each level of Looting adding 0-2 more.
          • The Drowned King now spawns in the sky around the world spawn point.

          • Improved the logic behind all griefing rules.
          • 🐞 Fixed items in item frames still being knocked loose by arrows while creeperGriefing was disabled.

          • 监守者刷怪蛋配方所需材料调整：幽匿催发体×4 -> 幽匿催发体×1 + 回响碎片×3。
          • 鞘翅配方所需材料调整：现在额外需要一个龙首。现在获得幻翼膜、龙首时将解锁配方。

          🐱 猫

          • 猫的礼物加入：枯萎的灌木。

          🔆 神匠

          • 燧灵火石的特殊效果现在也对永夜骑士、夜骑有效。

          🌸 进度

          • 偷梁换柱进度加入可选完成条件：嗅探兽刷怪蛋。
          • 回归自然进度加入条件：嗅探兽刷怪蛋。

          🐞 bug

          • 修复燧灵火石对应的挑战进度无法达成的问题。
          • 修复初版虚无地图机制残留的问题。

          🌊 核心特性

          • 今為監守、海衛、古海衛所誅之生靈，視為為戲者所誅。

          🌙 永恆之夜

          • 今疆階升至百階後度過之首夜，必為永恆之夜。
          • 今永恆之夜降後，度過零至六戲日後再降，不復為機率之事。

          🌾 長夜騎士

          • 今長夜騎士、夜騎·劫獸追界之蠹之所在時，會主擊監守、海衛、古海衛。

          📚 製物譜

          • 已增嗅獸孳卵之譜，所需之材為：炬蓮種二枚 + 小人蘭莢二枚。得材時解其譜。
          • 監守孳卵譜所需之材已調：幽匿引四枚 -> 幽匿引一枚 + 回音殘片三枚。
          • 翼譜所需之材已調：今另須一龍首。今得魘靈膜、龍首時解其譜。

          🐱 貓

          • 貓贈增：枯木。

          🔆 天工

          • 燧靈鐮之特效今亦生效於長夜騎士、夜騎。

          🌸 進程

          • 偷樑換柱進程增可擇之達條件：嗅獸孳卵。
          • 復歸自然進程增條件：嗅獸孳卵。

          🐞 bug

          • 已修燧靈鐮所對之挑戰進程弗能達之弊。
          • 已修初版太虛輿圖之機殘留之弊。

          🌊 Core Features

          • Mobs defeated by Wardens, Guardians, and Elder Guardians now count as defeated by a player.

          🌙 Eternal Night

          • The first night after reaching border level 100 is now always an Eternal Night.
          • After an Eternal Night arrives, the next one now arrives 0-6 game days later instead of being a chance-based event.

          🌾 Evernight Knights

          • Evernight Knights and Ravager Riders chasing a World Bug target now counterattack Wardens, Guardians, and Elder Guardians.

          📚 Crafting

          • Added the Sniffer Spawn Egg recipe, which costs Torchflower Seeds x2 + Pitcher Pod x2. It unlocks once the materials are obtained.
          • The Warden Spawn Egg recipe now costs Sculk Catalyst x1 + Echo Shard x3, down from Sculk Catalyst x4.
          • The Elytra recipe now additionally requires a Dragon Head. It unlocks once a Phantom Membrane and Dragon Head are obtained.

          • Cat gifts now include: Dead Bush.

          🔆 Chaos Smith

          • The Spirit Flint and Steel effect now also works on Evernight Knights and Night Riders.

          🌸 Advancements

          • The Back to Nature advancement now requires a Sniffer Spawn Egg.

          🐞 bug

          • Fixed the challenge advancement tied to the Spirit Flint and Steel being impossible to complete.
          • Fixed leftover mechanics from the first version of the Void map.

          • 除苦力怕外，其他怪物在试图攻击世界之虫目标时，不再具有视野穿透。(避免夜屿苍园、护樱山地图中具有远程攻击的怪物抛射物过多，导致弓箭声过于嘈杂久而久之可能造成的生理不适)

          • 除伏臨外，諸敵獸於圖擊界之蠹之所在時，不復具視貫穿。（免夜嶼蒼園、護櫻山輿圖中具遠擊之敵獸，其擲物過多，致弓矢之聲過於喧囂，久而久之或致生理不適）
          • 今伏臨圖擊界之蠹之所在而遭阻尋路時，若距之不足八方則燃，不復為十六方。

          🌊 Core Features

          • Except for creepers, monsters no longer have walls-of-sight while trying to attack a World Bug target. (This avoids the excessive projectile noise from ranged mobs in Night Garden and Cherry Blossom Mountain maps, which can become physically uncomfortable over time.)
          • A creeper trying to attack a World Bug target now ignites when within 8 blocks of it and its path is blocked, down from 16 blocks.

          🫑 10.7 1.21.1 2024-11-2810.7 1.21.1 2024-11-2810.7 1.21.1 2024-11-28

          🌊 核心特性

          • 当在昼夜模式的夜晚进行边界升级时，现在也会有过渡时间。

          • 优化了昼夜模式和禁锢模式的边界调整逻辑，以提高显示效果的整体稳定性。
          • 优化了在暂停时昼夜模式的边界调整逻辑，以修复边界颜色逻辑紊乱的问题。
          • 优化了禁锢模式和昼夜模式的BOSS栏逻辑，以提高性能。
          • 修复了当永夜骑士被和平模式清除时，BOSS栏会空槽的问题。

          • 永夜骑士与夜骑现在不会进入任何载具。
          • 永夜骑士与夜骑现在不会进入传送门。
          • 现在当永恒之夜结束后，夜骑-僵尸马 和 夜骑-骷髅马 会失去夜骑身份，失去夜骑身份后，不再享有持续恢复生命值效果，不会参与下一轮永恒之夜的降临，可以进入传送门。
          • 现在当 夜骑-劫掠兽 和 夜骑-幻翼 被玩家击败后会掉落8个随机战利品箱。(现在击败永夜军团后，累计可获得64个随机战利品箱与5个宝藏箱)

          🔆 神匠

          • 混沌之星特殊效果现在为生命恢复Ⅱ，不再是力量Ⅱ。

          🌸 进度

          • 调整了8000级和10000级的挑战进度描述文本。
          • 达成1000级以上挑战进度时，现在将获得100-1000经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 神一样的感觉、这又是什么？ 挑战进度时，现在将获得30经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 怪物收藏家、回归自然 挑战进度时，现在将获得50经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 金、钻、合金等无损灵材 的挑战进度时，现在将获得30-50经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 独立灵器 的挑战进度时，现在将获得40经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 钻灵独立灵器 的挑战进度时，现在将获得60经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 合金独立灵器 的挑战进度时，现在将获得80经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 灵器套装 的挑战进度时，现在将获得60-200经验等级奖励。
          • 达成 源返初归 挑战进度时，现在将获得100经验等级奖励。

          🌊 核心特性

          • 於晝夜之制之夜行世疆晉階時，今亦有過之時。
          • 禁錮之制之世疆調今不復受 doDaylightCycle 之則時暫之所制。
          • 已剔晝夜之制與禁錮之制之世疆調之理，以增高其顯之整穩。
          • 已剔暫息時晝夜之制之世疆調之理，以修世疆色之理紊亂。
          • 已剔禁錮之制與晝夜之制之BOSS欄之理，以增效能。
          • 已修長夜騎士為和之制所清時，BOSS欄空槽之弊。

          🌙 永恆之夜

          • 長夜騎士與夜騎今不入任何載具。
          • 長夜騎士與夜騎今不入結界門。
          • 今永恆之夜既終之後，夜騎-殭馬 與 夜騎-骷髏馬 失其夜騎之身；失其身後，不復享恆復命之效，不參下次永恆之夜之降，可入結界門。
          • 今 夜騎-劫獸 與 夜騎-魘靈 為戲者所誅後墜八個隨機貨箱。（今誅長夜軍團後，累可得六十四個隨機貨箱與五個寶櫝）

          🔆 天工

          • 鴻蒙珠之特效今為復甦Ⅱ，不復為力Ⅱ。

          🌸 進程

          • 已調八千階與一萬階之挑戰進程之述文。
          • 達一千階以上之挑戰進程時，今得一百至一千經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 神乎其神、此又是何物？ 挑戰進程時，今得三十經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 怪物博物、復歸自然 挑戰進程時，今得五十經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 金、金剛、玄鈺等無瑕靈材 之挑戰進程時，今得三十至五十經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 獨立靈器 之挑戰進程時，今得四十經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 金剛獨立靈器 之挑戰進程時，今得六十經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 玄鈺獨立靈器 之挑戰進程時，今得八十經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 靈器甲胄 之挑戰進程時，今得六十至二百經驗之階之賞。
          • 達 元歸本返 挑戰進程時，今得一百經驗之階之賞。

          🌊 Core Features

          • Border upgrades during nights in Day/Night Mode now have a transition period as well.
          • Border adjustments in Imprisoned Mode are no longer affected by the time pause of the doDaylightCycle rule.
          • Improved the border adjustment logic in Day/Night Mode and Imprisoned Mode for overall display stability.
          • Improved the Day/Night Mode border adjustment logic while paused, fixing scrambled border colors.
          • Improved the BOSS bar logic in Imprisoned Mode and Day/Night Mode for performance.
          • Fixed an empty BOSS bar slot when Peaceful Mode cleared the Evernight Knights.

          🌙 Eternal Night

          • Evernight Knights and Night Riders no longer enter any vehicle.
          • Evernight Knights and Night Riders no longer enter portals.
          • After an Eternal Night ends, the Night Rider on a Zombie Horse and the Night Rider on a Skeleton Horse lose their Night Rider status; once they lose it they no longer regenerate health continuously, do not take part in the next Eternal Night, and can enter portals.
          • Defeating a Ravager Rider or Phantom Rider as a player now drops 8 Random Loot Chests. (Defeating an Evernight Legion now yields 64 Random Loot Chests and 5 Loot Chests in total.)

          🔆 Chaos Smith

          • The Chaos Star effect is now Regeneration Ⅱ, down from Power Ⅱ.

          🌸 Advancements

          • Adjusted the description text of the level 8000 and 10000 challenge advancements.
          • Completing a challenge advancement at level 1000 or above now grants 100-1000 experience levels.
          • Completing the Feeling Divine or What Is This Now? challenge advancements now grants 30 experience levels.
          • Completing the Monster Collector or Back to Nature challenge advancements now grants 50 experience levels.
          • Completing the Unbreakable Spirit Wood, Diamond, or Netherite challenge advancements now grants 30-50 experience levels.
          • Completing a standalone Unbreakable Spirit Weapon challenge advancement now grants 40 experience levels.
          • Completing the standalone Unbreakable Spirit Diamond Weapon challenge advancement now grants 60 experience levels.
          • Completing the standalone Unbreakable Spirit Netherite Weapon challenge advancement now grants 80 experience levels.
          • Completing an Unbreakable Spirit Armor Set challenge advancement now grants 60-200 experience levels.
          • Completing the Return to the Source challenge advancement now grants 100 experience levels.

          🫑 10.0.5 1.21.1 2024-09-2810.0.5 1.21.1 2024-09-2810.0.5 1.21.1 2024-09-28

          • Added the griefing-rule-fix mod, which splits vanilla griefing behaviors such as creeper explosions, endermen moving dirt, and zombies breaking doors into separate rules. It also keeps creeper explosions from destroying item frames and glow item frames while creeperGriefing is disabled.

          🔆 Chaos Smith

          • The Chaos Merchant was renamed to the Chaos Smith, and all related text was updated.
          • Summon cooldown adjusted: once every 10 minutes -> once per day (game day).
          • Added a trade to redeem a random Loot Chest.
          • The Unbreakable Spirit material needed for Unbreakable Spirit Netherite equipment adjusted: Unbreakable Spirit Netherite x1 -> Netherite Ingot x1.
          • Simplified the unbreakable material names to: Unbreakable Spirit Wood, Unbreakable Spirit Stone, Unbreakable Spirit Iron, Unbreakable Spirit Diamond, Unbreakable Spirit Netherite.
          • Updated the descriptions of all unbreakable materials and Unbreakable Spirit Equipment.

          🌸 Advancements

          • Added the "What Is This Now?" challenge advancement.
          • Adjusted the description text of some special effect equipment.

          📱 Mods

          • Added the sodium-extra mod.
          • Added the reeses_sodium_options mod.

          🐞 Bug Fixes

          • Fixed Evernight Knights spawning twice in a row under certain conditions.

          🌸 8.7.4 1.20.4 2024-04-278.7.4 1.20.4 2024-04-278.7.4 1.20.4 2024-04-27

          • 永夜骑士加入溺尸国王，溺尸国王死亡后会掉落一个国王的宝藏。其坐骑死亡后会掉落1个随机战利品箱，每级抢夺魔咒额外掉落1个。
          • 僵尸骑士、骷髅骑士、幻术法师死亡后会掉落1个随机战利品箱，不再掉落远古残骸。其中，僵尸骑士和骷髅骑士被击败后，每级抢夺魔咒额外掉落0-1个随机战利品箱，幻术法师被击败后额外掉落1个。
          • 僵尸马和骷髅马不再掉落随机战利品箱。
          • 永夜骑士随机降临机制更改为轮询降临，现在会按照僵尸骑士、骷髅骑士、幻术法师、溺尸国王军团的顺序依次降临。例如，当前永夜降临的是僵尸骑士，那么在下一次永夜降临的必定是骷髅骑士，以此类推。
          • 现在永夜骑士的坐骑可以享受「当周围不存在玩家时，持续恢复生命值」的效果，僵尸/骷髅骑士所骑乘的僵尸/骷髅马的生命值由100点调整为50点。(回滚到初版设定)
          • 磁石箱不再被视为宝藏。
          • 長夜騎士增溺屍冥王；溺屍冥王斃後墜一國王之寶。其乘斃後墜一個隨機貨箱，每階奪咒額墜一個。
          • 殭屍冥騎、流髑冥騎、幻術法師斃後各墜一個隨機貨箱，不復墜上古之骸。其中殭屍冥騎與流髑冥騎為所誅後，每階奪咒額墜零至一個隨機貨箱；幻術法師為所誅後額墜一個。
          • 殭馬與骷髏馬不復墜隨機貨箱。
          • 長夜騎士隨機降臨之機已更為輪降；今依殭屍冥騎、流髑冥騎、幻術法師、溺屍冥王軍團之序次降。例如今降者為殭屍冥騎，則下次所降必為流髑冥騎，餘皆仿此。
          • 今長夜騎士之乘可享「近旁無戲者時恆復命」之效；殭屍／流髑冥騎所乘之殭馬／骷髏馬之命值由一百點調為五十點。（回退至初版之置）
          • 礠石箱不復為寶。
          • The Drowned King joined the Evernight Knights, and drops a King's Treasure when killed. Their mount drops 1 Random Loot Chest when killed, with 1 more per level of Looting.
          • Zombie Knights, Skeleton Knights, and Illusioners now drop 1 Random Loot Chest instead of Ancient Debris. Zombie Knights and Skeleton Knights additionally drop 0-1 Random Loot Chests per level of Looting, and Illusioners drop 1 more.
          • Zombie Horses and Skeleton Horses no longer drop Random Loot Chests.
          • Evernight Knights now arrive in rotation rather than at random, following the order Zombie Knight, Skeleton Knight, Illusioner, Drowned King. If the current long night brings a Zombie Knight, the next one brings a Skeleton Knight, and so on.
          • Evernight Knight mounts now benefit from regenerating health while no players are nearby, and the Zombie and Skeleton Horses ridden by Zombie and Skeleton Knights went from 100 health to 50. (Reverted to the initial values.)
          • Lodestone chests are no longer treated as Loot Chests.

          🌸 8.3 1.20.4 2024-03-098.3 1.20.4 2024-03-098.3 1.20.4 2024-03-09

          🌙 昼夜模式 — 永夜

          • 昼夜模式新增永夜事件：永夜将会在即将白昼时触发，每个游戏日有1/7概率触发，若连续六日未触发，在第七日必定触发。永夜触发后，世界出生点周围将会出现异界的僵尸骑士和骷髅骑士。受僵尸骑士和骷髅骑士的影响，时间将会停留在他们降临的那一刻，并且世界将陷入雷暴之中。当他们周围不存在玩家时，将会持续恢复生命值。其他怪物会被僵尸骑士和骷髅骑士吸引，但依然会优先攻击玩家。只有将僵尸骑士和骷髅骑士全部击败，才可解除其对世界的影响。

          🌊 特性

          • 添加三叉戟合成配方，解锁材料为海晶碎片或海晶砂砾。添加鞘翅合成配方，解锁材料为幻翼膜。神商新增蜘蛛眼兑换绿宝石的交易项。移除了神商线兑换绿宝石的交易项。终极效率挑战进度经验奖励由30级下调至20级。奢侈挑战进度经验奖励由50级下调至30级。

          🌟 地图

          • 守护 - 护樱山 地图v5更新，修复了该地图每次进入存档时，可能会异常丢失一级边界等级的问题。❗ 护樱山地图中含有专属数据包，若您开图时使用的是旧版本护樱山，可以将新版地图包中datapacks目录中名为init的数据包替换到您的存档中进行更新。❗ 在替换之前请先彻底删除旧版存档中的init数据包后，再放入新版数据包，而不是直接覆盖所有文件！

          🌙 晝夜之制 — 長夜

          • 晝夜之制新增長夜之事：長夜將於將晝之時引，每戲日有七分之一之機引；若連六日未引，第七日必引。長夜引後，界生處之旁將現異界之殭屍冥騎與流髑冥騎。為殭屍冥騎與流髑冥騎所制，時將滯於其降臨之一刻，且界將陷於雷暴之中。當其近旁無戲者時，恆復其命。餘敵獸為殭屍冥騎與流髑冥騎所吸，然猶先擊戲者。唯將殭屍冥騎與流髑冥騎盡誅，方可解其於界之所制。

          🌊 特性

          • 已加三叉戟之譜，解之材為海磷殘片或海磷晶。已加翼之譜，解之材為魘靈膜。天工新增蛛目易祖母綠之商項。已除天工綫易祖母綠之商項。究極其效挑戰進程之經驗之賞由三十階降至二十階。窮奢極侈挑戰進程之經驗之賞由五十階降至三十階。

          🌟 輿圖

          • 守護 - 護櫻山 輿圖v5已更，修此輿圖每入存檔時，或異常失一階疆階之弊。❗ 護櫻山輿圖含專屬錄囊；若卿開圖時所用為舊版護櫻山，可將新版輿圖囊中datapacks目錄內名為init之錄囊替入卿之存檔以更新之。❗ 替之前請先盡刪舊版存檔中之init錄囊，而後置入新版錄囊，非徑直覆之一切案！

          🌙 Day/Night Mode — Long Night

          • Day/Night Mode gains the long night event: it triggers just before dawn with a 1/7 chance each game day, and always on the seventh day if it has not triggered for six days running. Once it triggers, Zombie Knights and Skeleton Knights from another realm appear around the world spawn point. Their influence freezes time at the moment they arrived and plunges the world into a thunderstorm. They regenerate health continuously while no players are nearby. Other mobs are drawn to them but still prioritize attacking players. Only defeating every Zombie Knight and Skeleton Knight lifts their influence over the world.

          🌊 Features

          • Added the Trident recipe, unlocked with a Prismarine Shard or Prismarine Crystals. Added the Elytra recipe, unlocked with a Phantom Membrane. Added a Chaos Smith trade converting a Spider Eye into an Emerald, and removed the trade converting String into an Emerald. The Peak Efficiency challenge advancement experience reward dropped from 30 levels to 20, and the Lavish challenge advancement reward dropped from 50 to 30.

          🌟 Maps

          • The Cherry Blossom Mountain - Cherry Valley map v5 was updated, fixing an occasional loss of one border level when entering the save. ❗ The map ships with a dedicated data pack. Anyone playing on an older Cherry Blossom Mountain can swap the init data pack from the new map's datapacks folder into their save to update. ❗ Delete the old init data pack from the save completely before placing the new one, rather than overwriting every file.

          • 现在兑换萝灵竿、诡异灵竿、海之祝福，所需无损橡木均为2个。

          🌸 进度

          • 添加再破极限挑战进度。
          • 移除了1万级以上的边界等级目标进度。
          • 调整了部分与边界等级相关目标进度的达成所需等级条件。
          • 现在裁灵剪、遂灵火石、等非九件套装备的所有其他工具进度均为挑战进度，达成后均可获得30级经验，翱灵王翅为50级。
          • 调整了部分套装挑战进度的经验奖励。
          • 修复了兑换海之祝福后不会触发对应进度与套装进度的问题。
          • 修复了通过边界升级获得的战利品箱无法触发对应进度的问题。

          🔮 模组

          • 加入[砂轮增强]grind-enchantments模组，可以在砂轮中消耗经验将附魔分离到书中。将此模组加入核心模组分类。
          • 重新加入[样式代码输入]Allow-Color-Mod模组，可以在游戏中通过 alt+小键盘167键 输入 § 颜色样式符号。

          🌊 特性

          • 豕靈易物池中，炎靈桿與金剛石之機增。
          • 豕靈易物池增獄疣，與炎靈桿、終眇玥、鐵粒同機。
          • 豕靈易物池增凋靈髑首，與金剛石、抗火劑、靈逸鐵鞾同機。

          🔆 天工

          • 今易一切無瑕靈塊所需之祖母綠皆為六十四個。可易次數調為八次（可經補貨重整）。
          • 易無瑕鐵塊／無瑕金塊今所需之鐵塊／金塊為九個。
          • 無瑕金剛已改為無瑕金剛石塊；易之所需為一個金剛石塊。
          • 無瑕玄鈺已改為無瑕玄鈺塊；易之所需為一個玄鈺塊。
          • 今易胡蘆菔靈竿、譎蕈靈竿、海之賜福，所需之無瑕柞木皆為二個。

          🌸 進程

          • 已增 再破極限 挑戰進程。
          • 已除一萬階以上之疆階目標進程。
          • 已調部分與疆階相關之目標進程之達所需階條。
          • 今裁靈鉸、燧靈鐮等非九件套之兵者，諸餘工具進程皆為挑戰進程，達後皆得三十階經驗；翱靈王翼為五十階。
          • 已調部分甲胄挑戰進程之經驗之賞。
          • 已修易海之賜福後不引其對應進程與甲胄進程之弊。
          • 已修經世疆晉階所獲之貨箱弗能引其對應進程之弊。

          🔮 機

          • 已加[礪增]grind-enchantments機，可於礪中耗經驗將淬靈分離入書。已將此機歸入核心之類。
          • 已重加[樣式代碼輸入]Allow-Color-Mod機，可於戲中經 alt+小鍵盤167鍵 輸 § 色之樣式符。

          🌊 Features

          • The chance of Blaze Rods and Diamonds in the Piglin bartering pool was raised.
          • Nether Wart was added to the Piglin bartering pool, at the same chance as Blaze Rods, Ender Pearls, and Iron Nuggets.
          • Wither Skeleton Skulls were added to the Piglin bartering pool, at the same chance as Diamonds, Potions of Fire Resistance, and Soul Speed iron boots.

          🔆 Chaos Smith

          • Every Unbreakable Spirit Block now costs 64 Emeralds. The number of purchases was adjusted to 8 (refreshable by restocking).
          • Unbreakable Spirit Iron and Gold Blocks now cost 9 Blocks of Iron and Gold respectively.
          • Unbreakable Spirit Diamond is now Unbreakable Spirit Diamond Block, costing 1 Block of Diamond.
          • Unbreakable Spirit Netherite is now Unbreakable Spirit Netherite Block, costing 1 Block of Netherite.
          • The Spirit Lure Rod, Warped Spirit Lure Rod, and Blessing of the Sea now cost 2 Unbreakable Spirit Oak Wood each.

          🌸 Advancements

          • Added the Break the Limit challenge advancement.
          • Removed the border level goal advancements above level 10000.
          • Adjusted the level requirements for some border level goal advancements.
          • Tools outside the nine-piece set, such as the Spirit Shears and Spirit Flint and Steel, now all use challenge advancements worth 30 experience levels, and the Soaring Spirit Wings worth 50.
          • Adjusted the experience rewards of some set challenge advancements.
          • Fixed the Blessing of the Sea not triggering its advancement and set advancement after redemption.
          • Fixed loot chests obtained through border upgrades not triggering their advancements.

          🔮 Mods

          • Added the grind-enchantments mod, which separates enchantments into books by spending experience in a Grindstone. It was placed in the core mods category.
          • Re-added the Allow-Color-Mod mod, which allows entering the § color code in game with alt+num keypad 167.

          九、資囊

          • 漢化資囊-Minecraft-Mod-Language-Package 已更至 220616 版

          I. Core Update

          • Updated Minecraft to 1.19.

          II. Core Overhaul

          • Refactored the project and optimized the overall border logic. Does it feel more scientific now?
          • Fixed the border position possibly going wrong after switching border modes while the game is paused and time is frozen.

          III. New Features

          • Random Loot Chests gained Ancient City and Ancient City Ice Box loot.
          • Added spawn egg recipes for 24 animals including Chicken, Pig, Sheep, Cow, Mooshroom, Horse, Mule, Donkey, Rabbit, Cod, Salmon, Tropical Fish, Pufferfish, Squid, Dolphin, Frog, Parrot, Cat, Allay, Fox, Llama, Wolf, Panda, and Polar Bear.

          IV. Advancements

          • Added the Back to Nature challenge advancement.
          • The Sacred Spirit Spring advancement was renamed to Feeling Divine.
          • The Cursed Spirit Spring advancement was renamed to The Utmost Torment.
          • The ??? advancement was renamed to What Is This? A Chest?

          V. Crafting

          • The Wither Skeleton Spawn Egg recipe materials adjusted: Wither Skeleton Skull x4 -> Wither Skeleton Skull x1 + Bone x2 + Coal x1.
          • Improved the dye placement of some spawn egg recipes, following the top-left and bottom-right solid colors and top-right and bottom-left pattern rule.
          • Improved the unlock conditions of some recipes to make them more sensible.

          VI. Chaos Smith

          • Random Loot Chest adjusted: price: Emerald x16 -> Emerald x8; purchase limit: 12 -> 8.

          VII. Improvements

          • Improved the sign text style.
          • Improved the Day/Night and Night/Day Mode advancement hints: Night Falls -> Time Until Night Falls; Day Falls -> Time Until Day Falls.

          VIII. Mods

          • Added the iris mod.
          • Added the sodium mod.
          • carpet now enables redstone lag optimization, renewable sand, and smooth client animations by default.

          IX. Resource Packs

          • Updated Minecraft-Mod-Language-Package to 220616.
